2026-10-24T05:36:07.9765719Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9766164Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9767565Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9768690Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9770090Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9771935Z #
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9772350Z # Usage:
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9772997Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9773982Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9774879Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9775770Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9776775Z #
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9777268Z set -eu
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9777545Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9777780Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9778227Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9778674Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9779152Z do
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9779547Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9780003Z         in
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9780427Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9780959Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9781582Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9782220Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9782644Z     esac
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9783032Z done
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9783287Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9783643Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9784390Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9784630Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9785139Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9785637Z then
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9786043Z     WEBUI_ADDRESS="0.0.0.0"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9786715Z fi

2026-10-24T05:36:07.9788943Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9789557Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9790216Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9790987Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9791578Z     #
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9792224Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9793057Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9793754Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9794494Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9795268Z     else
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9795981Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9797688Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9798922Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9799216Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9799692Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9800052Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9800361Z       fi
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9800603Z     fi
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9800777Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9800971Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9801539Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9802313Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9802703Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9803400Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9804110Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9804848Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9805362Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9805906Z     else
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9806392Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9807351Z     fi
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9807626Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9807954Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9808629Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9809090Z }
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9809343Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9809593Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9810476Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9814722Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9815628Z     timeout=60
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9816167Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9818091Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9818520Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9819289Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9820195Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9820978Z             exit 1
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9821456Z         fi
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9821915Z         sleep 1
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9822415Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9822967Z     done
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9823276Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9823856Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9825081Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9826007Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9827280Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2

2026-10-24T05:36:07.9886224Z #!/bin/sh
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9887043Z # Functions to be used with livesys-* scripts
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9887550Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9887818Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9888363Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9889117Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9889846Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9890398Z fi
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9890704Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9891094Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9891772Z strstr() {
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9892239Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9892749Z }
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9893022Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9893287Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9893819Z action() {
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9894241Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9894562Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9894787Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9895246Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9895802Z     shift
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9896210Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9897034Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9898136Z     else
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9899437Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9900012Z     fi
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9900410Z     rc=$?
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9900807Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9901240Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9901647Z }
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9901915Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9902288Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9902998Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9903683Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9904384Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9904831Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9905364Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9905824Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9906423Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9907702Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9908435Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9909143Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9909855Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9910599Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9911324Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9912157Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9912799Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9913192Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9913458Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9914152Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9914925Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9915413Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9915657Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9916129Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9916785Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9917420Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9917874Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9918324Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9918813Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9919288Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9919763Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9920223Z         ;;
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9920661Z esac
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9920964Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9921230Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9921772Z success() {
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9922426Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9923062Z     return 0
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9923468Z }
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9923760Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9924013Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9924500Z failure() {
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9924929Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9925395Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9926161Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9926999Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9927443Z }
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9927619Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9927771Z echo_success() {
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9928053Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9933606Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9937535Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9941307Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9947543Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9948197Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9948670Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9949148Z     return 0
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9949590Z }
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9949979Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9950233Z echo_failure() {
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9950751Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9951325Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9951834Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_FAILURE
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9952459Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "FAILED")"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9953052Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9953627Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9954071Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9954507Z     return 1
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9954916Z }
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9955181Z
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9955416Z fscheck() {
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9955864Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9956387Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9958674Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9959200Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9959752Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9960207Z         ext[432])
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9960664Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9961167Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9961727Z             ;;
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9962143Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9962581Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9963076Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9963627Z             ;;
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9964042Z         btrfs)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9964587Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9965283Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9965913Z             ;;
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9966328Z         xfs)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9967121Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9967712Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9968474Z             ;;
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9968880Z         f2fs)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9969319Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9969884Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9970429Z             ;;
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9970831Z         *)
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9971381Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9972087Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9972626Z     esac
2026-10-24T05:36:07.9973013Z }

2026-10-24T05:36:08.0027918Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0028332Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0028747Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0029267Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0029808Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0030266Z   fi
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0030430Z
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0030691Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0031310Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0031985Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0032636Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0033051Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0033573Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0034350Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0035011Z     fi
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0035364Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0035821Z   fi
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0036242Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0037190Z
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0037396Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0037962Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0038406Z     echo
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0038765Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0039494Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0040436Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0040830Z   fi
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0041289Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0042041Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0042617Z   fi
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0042787Z
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0042938Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0043418Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0044066Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0044797Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0045473Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0046038Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0046802Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0047271Z }
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0047443Z
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0047619Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0048083Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0048514Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0049184Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-24T05:36:08.0049935Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img

2026-10-24T05:36:08.0050625Z
